"Fiducial:Fiducial_1mm_Mask2mm", ]  for full in fps:     lib, name = full.split(":")     fp = None     for d in fp_dirs:         p = d / f"{lib}.pretty"         if p.exists():             fp = pcbnew.FootprintLoad(str(p), name)             if fp:                 break     if not fp:         print(f"MISSING: {full}")         continue     print(f"=== {full} ===")     for pad in fp.Pads():         pos = pad.GetPosition()         sz = pad.GetSize()         dr = pad.GetDrillSize()         print(f"  pad {pad.GetNumber()!r:4s} at ({pcbnew.ToMM(pos.x):6.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(pos.y):6.2f}) sz=({pcbnew.ToMM(sz.x):.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(sz.y):.2f}) dr=({pcbnew.ToMM(dr.x):.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(dr.y):.2f}) attr={pad.GetAttribute()}")     for layer_name, lid in [("F.CrtYd", pcbnew.F_CrtYd), ("F.Fab", pcbnew.F_Fab), ("F.SilkS", pcbnew.F_SilkS)]:         xs, ys = [], []         circles = []         for gi in fp.GraphicalItems():             if hasattr(gi, "GetText") or gi.GetLayer() != lid:                 continue             bb = gi.GetBoundingBox()             xs.extend([pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetLeft()), pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetRight())])             ys.extend([pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetTop()), pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetBottom())])             if hasattr(gi, "GetShape") and gi.GetShape() == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_CIRCLE:                 c = gi.GetCenter()                 circles.append((round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x),2), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y),2), round(pcbnew.ToMM(gi.GetRadius()),2)))         if xs:             print(f"  {layer_name:8s}: x=[{min(xs):.2f}, {max(xs):.2f}] y=[{min(ys):.2f}, {max(ys):.2f}] w={max(xs)-min(xs):.2f} h={max(ys)-min(ys):.2f} circles={circles}")     if fp.Zones():         for z in fp.Zones():             bb = z.GetBoundingBox()             print(f"  ZONE: x=[{pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetLeft()):.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetRight()):.2f}] y=[{pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetTop()):.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetBottom()):.2f}]") '` |

| 63 | `run_command` | Check flipped SMD and THT pad coordinates | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys, pcbnew sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") from pcbnew_layout_template import create_base_board, FP_DIR  board = create_base_board(pathlib.Path("/tmp/test_smd_orient.kicad_pcb"), 0, 0, 30, 108)  def chk(lib, name, x, y, rot, is_back):     fp = pcbnew.FootprintLoad(str(FP_DIR / f"{lib}.pretty"), name)     board.Add(fp)     fp.SetPosition(pcbnew.VECTOR2I(pcbnew.FromMM(x), pcbnew.FromMM(y)))     if is_back:         fp.Flip(fp.GetPosition(), False)     fp.SetOrientationDegrees(rot)     pads = [(p.GetNumber(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.GetPosition().x), 2), round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.GetPosition().y), 2)) for p in fp.Pads()]     print(f"{name} rot={rot} back={is_back}: {pads}")  chk("Diode_SMD", "D_SOD-123", 10, 10, 0.0, True) chk("Diode_SMD", "D_SOD-123", 10, 10, 180.0, True) chk("Package_TO_SOT_SMD", "SOT-23", 10, 10, 0.0, True) chk("Package_TO_SOT_SMD", "SOT-23", 10, 10, 180.0, True) chk("Capacitor_THT", "CP_Radial_D6.3mm_P2.50mm", 23.55, 27.75, 0.0, True) chk("Capacitor_THT", "CP_Radial_D6.3mm_P2.50mm", 26.05, 35.75, 180.0, True) chk("Capacitor_THT", "C_Rect_L7.2mm_W7.2mm_P5.00mm_FKS2_FKP2_MKS2_MKP2", 22.30, 51.00, 0.0, True) '` |

| 100 | `run_command` | Diagnose NET_RECT_SUM pad anchors and passability | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items)  for ref, pin in [("U1", "9"), ("R10", "2"), ("R12", "2"), ("R13", "1")]:     fp = fps[ref]     pad = fp.FindPadByNumber(pin)     nc = pad.GetNetCode()     gx, gy, cx, cy, pl = g._pad_grid_anchor(router, fp, pad)     ax, ay = router.to_mm(gx, gy)     print(f"{ref}.{pin}: pad=({cx:.2f},{cy:.2f}) anchor=({ax:.2f},{ay:.2f}) grid=({pl},{gx},{gy}) passable={router.is_passable(pl, gx, gy, nc)} can_via={router.can_place_via(gx, gy, nc)}")     for dl in (0, 1):         nbrs = [(dx, dy, router.is_passable(dl, gx+dx, gy+dy, nc)) for dy in (-1,0,1) for dx in (-1,0,1) if router.is_passable(dl, gx+dx, gy+dy, nc)]         print(f"  layer {dl} passable nbrs around anchor: {nbrs}") '` |
| 102 | `run_command` | Trace why NET_RECT_SUM failed to route | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  # Wrap router.route to log which net runs and which fails orig_route = g.StrictGridRouter.route def traced_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer=None):     res = orig_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer)     if res is None:         print("FAILED route net_code=", net_code, "starts=", [self.to_mm(x,y) for _,x,y in starts], "targets=", [self.to_mm(x,y) for _,x,y in list(targets)[:5]])     return res g.StrictGridRouter.route = traced_route  # Run main up to route components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items)  # Print order and run try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print("ERROR:", e)     # Check what blocked U1.9 (grid 75, 324)     for l in (0, 1):         print(f"Around U1.9 (75,324) layer {l}:")         for dy in range(-4, 5):             row = ""             for dx in range(-4, 8):                 gx, gy = 75+dx, 324+dy                 idx = gy * router.nx + gx                 if router.static_block[l][idx]: row += "#"                 elif router.owner[l][idx] != -1: row += str(router.owner[l][idx]%10)                 elif router.pad_net[l][idx] != -1: row += "P"                 else: row += "."             print(f"  dy={dy:2d}: {row}") '` |

| 112 | `run_command` | Diagnose NET_U1D_POS blockage around U1.12 | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  orig_route = g.StrictGridRouter.route def traced_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer=None):     res = orig_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer)     if res is None:         print("FAILED route net_code=", net_code, "starts=", [self.to_mm(x,y) for _,x,y in starts], "targets=", [self.to_mm(x,y) for _,x,y in list(targets)[:5]])     return res g.StrictGridRouter.route = traced_route  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items) try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print("ERROR:", e)     for l in (0, 1):         print(f"Around U1.12 (75,339) layer {l}:")         for dy in range(-5, 6):             row = ""             for dx in range(-4, 10):                 gx, gy = 75+dx, 339+dy                 idx = gy * router.nx + gx                 if router.static_block[l][idx]: row += "#"                 elif router.owner[l][idx] != -1: row += chr(65 + (router.owner[l][idx]%26))                 elif router.pad_net[l][idx] != -1: row += "p"                 else: row += "."             print(f"  dy={dy:2d}: {row}") '` |
| 114 | `run_command` | Diagnose R17.2 blockage on NET_U1D_POS | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  orig_route = g.StrictGridRouter.route def traced_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer=None):     res = orig_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer)     if res is None:         print("FAILED route net_code=", net_code)     return res g.StrictGridRouter.route = traced_route  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items) try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print("ERROR:", e)     for l in (0, 1):         print(f"Around R17.2 (109,340) layer {l}:")         for dy in range(-12, 13):             row = ""             for dx in range(-12, 12):                 gx, gy = 109+dx, 340+dy                 if gx < 0 or gx >= router.nx or gy < 0 or gy >= router.ny:                     row += " "                     continue                 idx = gy * router.nx + gx                 if router.static_block[l][idx]: row += "#"                 elif router.owner[l][idx] != -1: row += chr(65 + (router.owner[l][idx]%26))                 elif router.pad_net[l][idx] != -1: row += "p"                 else: row += "."             print(f"  dy={dy:3d}: {row}") '` |
| 116 | `run_command` | Check BFS reachability of U1.12 and R17.2 | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  orig_route = g.StrictGridRouter.route components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items)  # Route until failure try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError:     pass  nc = net_items["NET_U1D_POS"].GetNetCode() for name, (l, sx, sy) in [("U1.12", (1, 75, 339)), ("R17.2", (1, 109, 340))]:     # BFS reachable count from (l, sx, sy)     q = [(l, sx, sy)]     seen = {(l, sx, sy)}     while q:         cl, cx, cy = q.pop()         for dx, dy in ((1,0),(-1,0),(0,1),(0,-1)):             nx_i, ny_i = cx+dx, cy+dy             if (cl, nx_i, ny_i) not in seen and router.is_passable(cl, nx_i, ny_i, nc):                 seen.add((cl, nx_i, ny_i)); q.append((cl, nx_i, ny_i))         if router.can_place_via(cx, cy, nc) and router.is_passable(1-cl, cx, cy, nc) and (1-cl, cx, cy) not in seen:             seen.add((1-cl, cx, cy)); q.append((1-cl, cx, cy))     xs = [x for _,x,_ in seen]; ys = [y for _,_,y in seen]     print(f"{name}: reachable={len(seen)}, x=[{min(xs)},{max(xs)}], y=[{min(ys)},{max(ys)}]") '` |

| 120 | `run_command` | Lint, format, and run generate_kicad_pcb.py | `ruff check --fix generate_kicad_pcb.py && ruff format generate_kicad_pcb.py && ruff check generate_kicad_pcb.py && ruff format --check generate_kicad_pcb.py && python3 generate_kicad_pcb.py` |
| 122 | `run_command` | Check reachability of NET_RECT_OUT pads | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items) try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print(e)  nc = net_items["NET_RECT_OUT"].GetNetCode() for ref, pin in [("U1", "8"), ("R14", "2"), ("R15", "1"), ("TP5", "1")]:     fp = fps[ref]     pad = fp.FindPadByNumber(pin)     if (ref, pin) in ic_anchors:         l, gx, gy = ic_anchors[(ref, pin)]     else:         gx, gy, _, _, l = g._pad_grid_anchor(router, fp, pad)     q = [(l, gx, gy)]     seen = {(l, gx, gy)}     while q:         cl, cx, cy = q.pop()         for dx, dy in ((1,0),(-1,0),(0,1),(0,-1)):             nx_i, ny_i = cx+dx, cy+dy             if (cl, nx_i, ny_i) not in seen and router.is_passable(cl, nx_i, ny_i, nc):                 seen.add((cl, nx_i, ny_i)); q.append((cl, nx_i, ny_i))         is_tht = router.tht_pad_grid.get((cx, cy)) == nc         if (is_tht or router.can_place_via(cx, cy, nc)) and router.is_passable(1-cl, cx, cy, nc) and (1-cl, cx, cy) not in seen:             seen.add((1-cl, cx, cy)); q.append((1-cl, cx, cy))     xs = [x for _,x,_ in seen]; ys = [y for _,_,y in seen]     print(f"{ref}.{pin} ({router.to_mm(gx,gy)}): reachable={len(seen)}, x=[{min(xs)},{max(xs)}], y=[{min(ys)},{max(ys)}]") '` |
| 124 | `run_command` | Print ASCII map around U1.8 on F.Cu and B.Cu | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items) try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError:     pass  for l in (0, 1):     print(f"Around U1.8 (75,319) layer {l}:")     for dy in range(-12, 10):         row = ""         for dx in range(-6, 12):             gx, gy = 75+dx, 319+dy             idx = gy * router.nx + gx             if router.static_block[l][idx]: row += "#"             elif router.owner[l][idx] != -1: row += chr(65 + (router.owner[l][idx]%26))             elif router.pad_net[l][idx] != -1: row += "p"             else: row += "."         print(f"  dy={dy:3d}: {row}") '` |

| 171 | `run_command` | Check reachability of NET_PREAMP_OUT pads | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes(board, router, fps, net_items) try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print(e)  nc = net_items["NET_PREAMP_OUT"].GetNetCode() for ref, pin in [("U1", "1"), ("R6", "2"), ("C8", "2"), ("R8", "1"), ("R11", "1"), ("TP4", "1")]:     fp = fps[ref]     pad = fp.FindPadByNumber(pin)     if (ref, pin) in ic_anchors:         l, gx, gy = ic_anchors[(ref, pin)]     else:         gx, gy, _, _, l = g._pad_grid_anchor(router, fp, pad)     q = [(l, gx, gy)]     seen = {(l, gx, gy)}     while q:         cl, cx, cy = q.pop()         for dx, dy in ((1,0),(-1,0),(0,1),(0,-1)):             nx_i, ny_i = cx+dx, cy+dy             if (cl, nx_i, ny_i) not in seen and router.is_passable(cl, nx_i, ny_i, nc):                 seen.add((cl, nx_i, ny_i)); q.append((cl, nx_i, ny_i))         is_tht = router.tht_pad_grid.get((cx, cy)) == nc         if (is_tht or router.can_place_via(cx, cy, nc)) and router.is_passable(1-cl, cx, cy, nc) and (1-cl, cx, cy) not in seen:             seen.add((1-cl, cx, cy)); q.append((1-cl, cx, cy))     xs = [x for _,x,_ in seen]; ys = [y for _,_,y in seen]     print(f"{ref}.{pin} ({router.to_mm(gx,gy)}): reachable={len(seen)}, x=[{min(xs)},{max(xs)}], y=[{min(ys)},{max(ys)}]") '` |

| 196 | `run_command` | Inspect full DRC violations and parity summary | `python3 -c ' import json d = json.load(open("/tmp/drc.json")) print("violations:", len(d.get("violations", [])), "unconnected:", len(d.get("unconnected_items", [])), "schematic_parity:", len(d.get("schematic_parity", []))) for v in d.get("violations", []):     print("VIOL:", v.get("type"), v.get("severity"), v.get("description")) for u in d.get("unconnected_items", []):     print("UNCONN:", u.get("type"), u.get("description"), [i.get("description") for i in u.get("items", [])]) ' && echo "--- parity types ---" && python3 -c ' import json from collections import Counter d = json.load(open("/tmp/drc.json")) print(Counter(p.get("type") for p in d.get("schematic_parity", []))) for p in d.get("schematic_parity", [])[:10]:     print(" ", p.get("type"), p.get("description")) '` |
| 198 | `run_command` | Print exact DRC violation locations | `python3 -c ' import json d = json.load(open("/tmp/drc.json")) for idx, v in enumerate(d.get("violations", [])):     print(f"[{idx}] {v.get(\"type\")} ({v.get(\"severity\")}): {v.get(\"description\")}")     for it in v.get("items", []):         print("   ", it.get("description"), it.get("pos")) for idx, u in enumerate(d.get("unconnected_items", [])):     print(f"[UNCONN {idx}] {u.get(\"description\")}")     for it in u.get("items", []):         print("   ", it.get("description"), it.get("pos")) '` |
| 200 | `run_command` | Print exact DRC violation locations | `python3 -c ' import json d = json.load(open("/tmp/drc.json")) for idx, v in enumerate(d.get("violations", [])):     t, s, desc = v.get("type"), v.get("severity"), v.get("description")     print(f"[{idx}] {t} ({s}): {desc}")     for it in v.get("items", []):         print("   ", it.get("description"), it.get("pos")) for idx, u in enumerate(d.get("unconnected_items", [])):     desc = u.get("description")     print(f"[UNCONN {idx}] {desc}")     for it in u.get("items", []):         print("   ", it.get("description"), it.get("pos")) '` |
| 202 | `run_command` | Run analyze_pcb and cross_analysis on generated PCB | `python3 .agents/skills/kicad/scripts/analyze_pcb.py envelope_follower.kicad_pcb --full --schematic /tmp/sch_check.json --output /tmp/pcb_analysis.json && python3 .agents/skills/kicad/scripts/cross_analysis.py --schematic /tmp/sch_check.json --pcb /tmp/pcb_analysis.json --output /tmp/cross_analysis.json && python3 -c ' import json p = json.load(open("/tmp/pcb_analysis.json")) print("=== PCB ANALYSIS ===") print("summary:", p["summary"]) print("stats:", {k: p["statistics"].get(k) for k in ("footprint_count", "smd_count", "tht_count", "via_count", "zone_count", "routing_complete", "unrouted_net_count")}) print("fab_notes:", p.get("fab_notes_completeness")) for f in p.get("findings", []):     if f.get("severity") != "info" or f.get("rule_id") in ("VP-001", "BV-001", "FD-001", "OR-001", "SK-001", "TE-001", "CP-001"):         print(f"  [{f.get(\"severity\")}] {f.get(\"rule_id\")}: {f.get(\"summary\")}") c = json.load(open("/tmp/cross_analysis.json")) print("=== CROSS ANALYSIS ===") print("summary:", c["summary"]) for f in c.get("findings", []):     if f.get("severity") != "info" or f.get("rule_id", "").startswith("XV-"):         print(f"  [{f.get(\"severity\")}] {f.get(\"rule_id\")}: {f.get(\"summary\")}") '` |

| 244 | `run_command` | Check reachability of NET_PREAMP_OUT when NET_U1A_POS is in tier 0 | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes(board, router, fps, net_items) try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print(e)  nc = net_items["NET_PREAMP_OUT"].GetNetCode() for ref, pin in [("U1", "1"), ("R6", "2"), ("C8", "2"), ("R8", "1"), ("R11", "1"), ("TP4", "1")]:     fp = fps[ref]     pad = fp.FindPadByNumber(pin)     if (ref, pin) in ic_anchors:         l, gx, gy = ic_anchors[(ref, pin)]     else:         gx, gy, _, _, l = g._pad_grid_anchor(router, fp, pad)     q = [(l, gx, gy)]     seen = {(l, gx, gy)}     while q:         cl, cx, cy = q.pop()         for dx, dy in ((1,0),(-1,0),(0,1),(0,-1)):             nx_i, ny_i = cx+dx, cy+dy             if (cl, nx_i, ny_i) not in seen and router.is_passable(cl, nx_i, ny_i, nc):                 seen.add((cl, nx_i, ny_i)); q.append((cl, nx_i, ny_i))         is_tht = router.tht_pad_grid.get((cx, cy)) == nc         if (is_tht or router.can_place_via(cx, cy, nc)) and router.is_passable(1-cl, cx, cy, nc) and (1-cl, cx, cy) not in seen:             seen.add((1-cl, cx, cy)); q.append((1-cl, cx, cy))     xs = [x for _,x,_ in seen]; ys = [y for _,_,y in seen]     print(f"{ref}.{pin} ({router.to_mm(gx,gy)}): reachable={len(seen)}, x=[{min(xs)},{max(xs)}], y=[{min(ys)},{max(ys)}]") '` |

| 390 | `run_command` | List all silkscreen texts and shapes in PCB | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 layer_name = {pcbnew.F_SilkS: "F.SilkS", pcbnew.B_SilkS: "B.SilkS"}  print("=== BOARD DRAWINGS ON SILK ===") for d in b.GetDrawings():     l = d.GetLayer()     if l not in layer_name:         continue     if isinstance(d, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT):         p = d.GetPosition()         h = pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetTextHeight())         w = pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetTextThickness())         print(f"BOARD_TEXT {layer_name[l]} {d.GetText()!r:18} ({pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0:6.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0:6.2f}) deg={d.GetTextAngleDegrees():5.1f} h={h:.2f} w={w:.2f} mir={d.IsMirrored()}")     elif isinstance(d, pcbnew.PCB_SHAPE):         bb = d.GetBoundingBox()         print(f"BOARD_SHAPE {layer_name[l]} shape={d.GetShape()} ({pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetX())-X0:6.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetY())-Y0:6.2f})..({pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetRight())-X0:6.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetBottom())-Y0:6.2f})")  print("\n=== FOOTPRINT SILK TEXTS ===") for fp in sorted(b.GetFootprints(), key=lambda f: f.GetReference()):     ref_str = fp.GetReference()     rf = fp.Reference()     if rf.IsVisible() and rf.GetLayer() in layer_name:         p = rf.GetPosition()         print(f"FP_REF  {ref_str:6} {layer_name[rf.GetLayer()]} {rf.GetText()!r:14} ({pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0:6.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0:6.2f}) deg={rf.GetTextAngleDegrees():5.1f} h={pcbnew.ToMM(rf.GetTextHeight()):.2f}")     for gi in fp.GraphicalItems():         if isinstance(gi, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT) and gi.IsVisible() and gi.GetLayer() in layer_name:             p = gi.GetPosition()             print(f"FP_TXT  {ref_str:6} {layer_name[gi.GetLayer()]} {gi.GetText()!r:14} ({pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0:6.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0:6.2f}) deg={gi.GetTextAngleDegrees():5.1f} h={pcbnew.ToMM(gi.GetTextHeight()):.2f}") '` |
| 392 | `run_command` | Inspect PCB_SHAPE geometry getters on silk layers | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for d in b.GetDrawings():     if d.GetLayer() in (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS) and isinstance(d, pcbnew.PCB_SHAPE):         st = d.GetShape()         if st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_RECT:             s, e = d.GetStart(), d.GetEnd()             print("RECT", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.y)-Y0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.y)-Y0, 3), "filled=", d.IsFilled(), "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3))         elif st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_CIRCLE:             c = d.GetCenter()             print("CIRCLE", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y)-Y0, 3), "r=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetRadius()), 3), "filled=", d.IsFilled(), "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3))         elif st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_SEGMENT:             s, e = d.GetStart(), d.GetEnd()             print("SEG", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.y)-Y0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.y)-Y0, 3), "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3)) '` |
| 394 | `run_command` | Verify KiCad 10 PCB_SHAPE getters | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for d in b.GetDrawings():     if d.GetLayer() in (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS) and isinstance(d, pcbnew.PCB_SHAPE):         st = d.GetShape()         filled = d.GetFillMode() != pcbnew.FILLED_SHAPE_NONE if hasattr(pcbnew, "FILLED_SHAPE_NONE") else d.IsSolidFill()         if st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_RECT:             s, e = d.GetStart(), d.GetEnd()             print("RECT", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.y)-Y0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.y)-Y0, 3), "filled=", filled, "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3))         elif st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_CIRCLE:             c = d.GetCenter()             print("CIRCLE", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y)-Y0, 3), "r=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetRadius()), 3), "filled=", filled, "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3)) '` |
| 396 | `read_file` |  | [`silk_annotator.py`](file:///Users/brett/Documents/GitHub/EnvelopeFollowerWithSkewLimiter/.agents/skills/kicad-silkscreen-markup/scripts/silk_annotator.py) |
| 398 | `run_command` | Check KiCad 10 text angle and justify getters on rotated footprints | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for ref in ("RV1", "C2", "U1", "U2", "D2", "R1", "R2", "R14"):     fp = b.FindFootprintByReference(ref)     rf = fp.Reference()     print(f"{ref:4} fp_rot={fp.GetOrientationDegrees():6.1f} flipped={str(fp.IsFlipped()):5} \| REF vis={rf.IsVisible()} pos=({pcbnew.ToMM(rf.GetPosition().x)-X0:.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(rf.GetPosition().y)-Y0:.2f}) deg={rf.GetTextAngleDegrees():.1f} mir={rf.IsMirrored()}")     for gi in fp.GraphicalItems():         if isinstance(gi, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT) and gi.GetLayer() in (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS):             p = gi.GetPosition()             print(f"     TXT {gi.GetText()!r:10} pos=({pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0:.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0:.2f}) deg={gi.GetTextAngleDegrees():.1f} mir={gi.IsMirrored()} hjust={gi.GetHorizJustify()} vjust={gi.GetVertJustify()}") '` |
| 400 | `run_command` | Compare PCB_TEXT position vs bounding box center | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for ref in ("RV1", "U1", "U2", "D2", "R1"):     fp = b.FindFootprintByReference(ref)     for gi in fp.GraphicalItems():         if isinstance(gi, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT) and gi.GetLayer() in (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS):             p = gi.GetPosition()             c = gi.GetBoundingBox().GetCenter()             print(ref, gi.GetText(), "pos=", (round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0,3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0,3)), "bb_ctr=", (round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x)-X0,3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y)-Y0,3))) '` |
| 402 | `run_command` | Compare Reference PCB_FIELD position vs bbox center | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for ref in ("RV1", "U1", "U2", "D2", "J1", "SW2"):     rf = b.FindFootprintByReference(ref).Reference()     p = rf.GetPosition()     c = rf.GetBoundingBox().GetCenter()     print(ref, "pos=", (round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0,3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0,3)), "bb_ctr=", (round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x)-X0,3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y)-Y0,3))) '` |

"Package_TO_SOT_SMD:SOT-23",     "Resistor_SMD:R_0603_1608Metric",     "Capacitor_SMD:C_0603_1608Metric",     "Capacitor_SMD:C_0805_2012Metric",     "Capacitor_SMD:C_1206_3216Metric",     "Capacitor_THT:CP_Radial_D6.3mm_P2.50mm",     "Capacitor_THT:C_Rect_L7.2mm_W4.5mm_P5.00mm_FKS2_FKP2_MKS2_MKP2",     "Capacitor_THT:C_Rect_L7.2mm_W7.2mm_P5.00mm_FKS2_FKP2_MKS2_MKP2",     "Potentiometer_THT:Potentiometer_Alpha_RD901F-40-00D_Single_Vertical",     "Eurorack_Custom:SW_Toggle_SPDT_Dailywell_2MS1",     "LED_THT:LED_D3.0mm",     "Connector_Audio:Jack_3.5mm_QingPu_WQP-PJ398SM_Vertical_CircularHoles",     "Connector_IDC:IDC-Header_2x05_P2.54mm_Vertical",     "TestPoint:TestPoint_Keystone_5000-5004_Miniature",     "Fiducial:Fiducial_1mm_Mask2mm", ]  for full in fps:     lib, name = full.split(":")     fp = None     for d in fp_dirs:         p = d / f"{lib}.pretty"         if p.exists():             fp = pcbnew.FootprintLoad(str(p), name)             if fp:                 break     if not fp:         print(f"MISSING: {full}")         continue     print(f"=== {full} ===")     for pad in fp.Pads():         pos = pad.GetPosition()         sz = pad.GetSize()         dr = pad.GetDrillSize()         print(f"  pad {pad.GetNumber()!r:4s} at ({pcbnew.ToMM(pos.x):6.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(pos.y):6.2f}) sz=({pcbnew.ToMM(sz.x):.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(sz.y):.2f}) dr=({pcbnew.ToMM(dr.x):.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(dr.y):.2f}) attr={pad.GetAttribute()}")     for layer_name, lid in [("F.CrtYd", pcbnew.F_CrtYd), ("F.Fab", pcbnew.F_Fab), ("F.SilkS", pcbnew.F_SilkS)]:         xs, ys = [], []         circles = []         for gi in fp.GraphicalItems():             if hasattr(gi, "GetText") or gi.GetLayer() != lid:                 continue             bb = gi.GetBoundingBox()             xs.extend([pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetLeft()), pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetRight())])             ys.extend([pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetTop()), pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetBottom())])             if hasattr(gi, "GetShape") and gi.GetShape() == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_CIRCLE:                 c = gi.GetCenter()                 circles.append((round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x),2), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y),2), round(pcbnew.ToMM(gi.GetRadius()),2)))         if xs:             print(f"  {layer_name:8s}: x=[{min(xs):.2f}, {max(xs):.2f}] y=[{min(ys):.2f}, {max(ys):.2f}] w={max(xs)-min(xs):.2f} h={max(ys)-min(ys):.2f} circles={circles}")     if fp.Zones():         for z in fp.Zones():             bb = z.GetBoundingBox() | 63 | Check flipped SMD and THT pad coordinates | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys, pcbnew sys.path.insert(0, ".agents/skills/kicad-pcbnew-layout/scripts") from pcbnew_layout_template import create_base_board, FP_DIR  board = create_base_board(pathlib.Path("/tmp/test_smd_orient.kicad_pcb"), 0, 0, 30, 108)  def chk(lib, name, x, y, rot, is_back):     fp = pcbnew.FootprintLoad(str(FP_DIR / f"{lib}.pretty"), name)     board.Add(fp)     fp.SetPosition(pcbnew.VECTOR2I(pcbnew.FromMM(x), pcbnew.FromMM(y)))     if is_back:         fp.Flip(fp.GetPosition(), False)     fp.SetOrientationDegrees(rot)     pads = [(p.GetNumber(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.GetPosition().x), 2), round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.GetPosition().y), 2)) for p in fp.Pads()]     print(f"{name} rot={rot} back={is_back}: {pads}")  chk("Diode_SMD", "D_SOD-123", 10, 10, 0.0, True) chk("Diode_SMD", "D_SOD-123", 10, 10, 180.0, True) chk("Package_TO_SOT_SMD", "SOT-23", 10, 10, 0.0, True) chk("Package_TO_SOT_SMD", "SOT-23", 10, 10, 180.0, True) chk("Capacitor_THT", "CP_Radial_D6.3mm_P2.50mm", 23.55, 27.75, 0.0, True) chk("Capacitor_THT", "CP_Radial_D6.3mm_P2.50mm", 26.05, 35.75, 180.0, True) chk("Capacitor_THT", "C_Rect_L7.2mm_W7.2mm_P5.00mm_FKS2_FKP2_MKS2_MKP2", 22.30, 51.00, 0.0, True) '` |

| 100 | Diagnose NET_RECT_SUM pad anchors and passability | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items)  for ref, pin in [("U1", "9"), ("R10", "2"), ("R12", "2"), ("R13", "1")]:     fp = fps[ref]     pad = fp.FindPadByNumber(pin)     nc = pad.GetNetCode()     gx, gy, cx, cy, pl = g._pad_grid_anchor(router, fp, pad)     ax, ay = router.to_mm(gx, gy)     print(f"{ref}.{pin}: pad=({cx:.2f},{cy:.2f}) anchor=({ax:.2f},{ay:.2f}) grid=({pl},{gx},{gy}) passable={router.is_passable(pl, gx, gy, nc)} can_via={router.can_place_via(gx, gy, nc)}")     for dl in (0, 1):         nbrs = [(dx, dy, router.is_passable(dl, gx+dx, gy+dy, nc)) for dy in (-1,0,1) for dx in (-1,0,1) if router.is_passable(dl, gx+dx, gy+dy, nc)]         print(f"  layer {dl} passable nbrs around anchor: {nbrs}") '` |
| 102 | Trace why NET_RECT_SUM failed to route | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  # Wrap router.route to log which net runs and which fails orig_route = g.StrictGridRouter.route def traced_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer=None):     res = orig_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer)     if res is None:         print("FAILED route net_code=", net_code, "starts=", [self.to_mm(x,y) for _,x,y in starts], "targets=", [self.to_mm(x,y) for _,x,y in list(targets)[:5]])     return res g.StrictGridRouter.route = traced_route  # Run main up to route components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items)  # Print order and run try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print("ERROR:", e)     # Check what blocked U1.9 (grid 75, 324)     for l in (0, 1):         print(f"Around U1.9 (75,324) layer {l}:")         for dy in range(-4, 5):             row = ""             for dx in range(-4, 8):                 gx, gy = 75+dx, 324+dy                 idx = gy * router.nx + gx                 if router.static_block[l][idx]: row += "#"                 elif router.owner[l][idx] != -1: row += str(router.owner[l][idx]%10)                 elif router.pad_net[l][idx] != -1: row += "P"                 else: row += "."             print(f"  dy={dy:2d}: {row}") '` |

| 114 | Diagnose R17.2 blockage on NET_U1D_POS | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  orig_route = g.StrictGridRouter.route def traced_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer=None):     res = orig_route(self, starts, targets, net_code, preferred_layer)     if res is None:         print("FAILED route net_code=", net_code)     return res g.StrictGridRouter.route = traced_route  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items) try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print("ERROR:", e)     for l in (0, 1):         print(f"Around R17.2 (109,340) layer {l}:")         for dy in range(-12, 13):             row = ""             for dx in range(-12, 12):                 gx, gy = 109+dx, 340+dy                 if gx < 0 or gx >= router.nx or gy < 0 or gy >= router.ny:                     row += " "                     continue                 idx = gy * router.nx + gx                 if router.static_block[l][idx]: row += "#"                 elif router.owner[l][idx] != -1: row += chr(65 + (router.owner[l][idx]%26))                 elif router.pad_net[l][idx] != -1: row += "p"                 else: row += "."             print(f"  dy={dy:3d}: {row}") '` |
| 116 | Check BFS reachability of U1.12 and R17.2 | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  orig_route = g.StrictGridRouter.route components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items)  # Route until failure try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError:     pass  nc = net_items["NET_U1D_POS"].GetNetCode() for name, (l, sx, sy) in [("U1.12", (1, 75, 339)), ("R17.2", (1, 109, 340))]:     # BFS reachable count from (l, sx, sy)     q = [(l, sx, sy)]     seen = {(l, sx, sy)}     while q:         cl, cx, cy = q.pop()         for dx, dy in ((1,0),(-1,0),(0,1),(0,-1)):             nx_i, ny_i = cx+dx, cy+dy             if (cl, nx_i, ny_i) not in seen and router.is_passable(cl, nx_i, ny_i, nc):                 seen.add((cl, nx_i, ny_i)); q.append((cl, nx_i, ny_i))         if router.can_place_via(cx, cy, nc) and router.is_passable(1-cl, cx, cy, nc) and (1-cl, cx, cy) not in seen:             seen.add((1-cl, cx, cy)); q.append((1-cl, cx, cy))     xs = [x for _,x,_ in seen]; ys = [y for _,_,y in seen]     print(f"{name}: reachable={len(seen)}, x=[{min(xs)},{max(xs)}], y=[{min(ys)},{max(ys)}]") '` |

| 122 | Check reachability of NET_RECT_OUT pads | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes_and_decoupling(board, router, fps, net_items) try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print(e)  nc = net_items["NET_RECT_OUT"].GetNetCode() for ref, pin in [("U1", "8"), ("R14", "2"), ("R15", "1"), ("TP5", "1")]:     fp = fps[ref]     pad = fp.FindPadByNumber(pin)     if (ref, pin) in ic_anchors:         l, gx, gy = ic_anchors[(ref, pin)]     else:         gx, gy, _, _, l = g._pad_grid_anchor(router, fp, pad)     q = [(l, gx, gy)]     seen = {(l, gx, gy)}     while q:         cl, cx, cy = q.pop()         for dx, dy in ((1,0),(-1,0),(0,1),(0,-1)):             nx_i, ny_i = cx+dx, cy+dy             if (cl, nx_i, ny_i) not in seen and router.is_passable(cl, nx_i, ny_i, nc):                 seen.add((cl, nx_i, ny_i)); q.append((cl, nx_i, ny_i))         is_tht = router.tht_pad_grid.get((cx, cy)) == nc         if (is_tht or router.can_place_via(cx, cy, nc)) and router.is_passable(1-cl, cx, cy, nc) and (1-cl, cx, cy) not in seen:             seen.add((1-cl, cx, cy)); q.append((1-cl, cx, cy))     xs = [x for _,x,_ in seen]; ys = [y for _,_,y in seen]     print(f"{ref}.{pin} ({router.to_mm(gx,gy)}): reachable={len(seen)}, x=[{min(xs)},{max(xs)}], y=[{min(ys)},{max(ys)}]") '` |

| 171 | Check reachability of NET_PREAMP_OUT pads | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pathlib, sys sys.path.insert(0, ".") import generate_kicad_pcb as g  components, nets = g.parse_netlist(g.NET_PATH, dnp_prefixes=("TP",)) sch_uuids = g.parse_schematic_unit1_uuids(g.SCH_PATH) board = g.create_base_board(g.PCB_PATH, g.BOARD_X0, g.BOARD_Y0, g.BOARD_X1, g.BOARD_Y1) fps = g.add_board_footprints(board, components, sch_uuids) net_items, _ = g.assign_nets(board, fps, nets) router = g._init_router(board, fps, net_items) g._place_smd_gnd_vias(board, router, fps, net_items["GND"]) ic_anchors = g._pre_route_ic_escapes(board, router, fps, net_items) try:     g._route_all_nets(board, router, fps, net_items, nets, ic_anchors) except RuntimeError as e:     print(e)  nc = net_items["NET_PREAMP_OUT"].GetNetCode() for ref, pin in [("U1", "1"), ("R6", "2"), ("C8", "2"), ("R8", "1"), ("R11", "1"), ("TP4", "1")]:     fp = fps[ref]     pad = fp.FindPadByNumber(pin)     if (ref, pin) in ic_anchors:         l, gx, gy = ic_anchors[(ref, pin)]     else:         gx, gy, _, _, l = g._pad_grid_anchor(router, fp, pad)     q = [(l, gx, gy)]     seen = {(l, gx, gy)}     while q:         cl, cx, cy = q.pop()         for dx, dy in ((1,0),(-1,0),(0,1),(0,-1)):             nx_i, ny_i = cx+dx, cy+dy             if (cl, nx_i, ny_i) not in seen and router.is_passable(cl, nx_i, ny_i, nc):                 seen.add((cl, nx_i, ny_i)); q.append((cl, nx_i, ny_i))         is_tht = router.tht_pad_grid.get((cx, cy)) == nc         if (is_tht or router.can_place_via(cx, cy, nc)) and router.is_passable(1-cl, cx, cy, nc) and (1-cl, cx, cy) not in seen:             seen.add((1-cl, cx, cy)); q.append((1-cl, cx, cy))     xs = [x for _,x,_ in seen]; ys = [y for _,_,y in seen]     print(f"{ref}.{pin} ({router.to_mm(gx,gy)}): reachable={len(seen)}, x=[{min(xs)},{max(xs)}], y=[{min(ys)},{max(ys)}]") '` |

| 198 | Print exact DRC violation locations | `python3 -c ' import json d = json.load(open("/tmp/drc.json")) for idx, v in enumerate(d.get("violations", [])):     print(f"[{idx}] {v.get(\"type\")} ({v.get(\"severity\")}): {v.get(\"description\")}")     for it in v.get("items", []):         print("   ", it.get("description"), it.get("pos")) for idx, u in enumerate(d.get("unconnected_items", [])):     print(f"[UNCONN {idx}] {u.get(\"description\")}")     for it in u.get("items", []):         print("   ", it.get("description"), it.get("pos")) '` |
| 200 | Print exact DRC violation locations | `python3 -c ' import json d = json.load(open("/tmp/drc.json")) for idx, v in enumerate(d.get("violations", [])):     t, s, desc = v.get("type"), v.get("severity"), v.get("description")     print(f"[{idx}] {t} ({s}): {desc}")     for it in v.get("items", []):         print("   ", it.get("description"), it.get("pos")) for idx, u in enumerate(d.get("unconnected_items", [])):     desc = u.get("description")     print(f"[UNCONN {idx}] {desc}")     for it in u.get("items", []):         print("   ", it.get("description"), it.get("pos")) '` |
| 202 | Run analyze_pcb and cross_analysis on generated PCB | `python3 .agents/skills/kicad/scripts/analyze_pcb.py envelope_follower.kicad_pcb --full --schematic /tmp/sch_check.json --output /tmp/pcb_analysis.json && python3 .agents/skills/kicad/scripts/cross_analysis.py --schematic /tmp/sch_check.json --pcb /tmp/pcb_analysis.json --output /tmp/cross_analysis.json && python3 -c ' import json p = json.load(open("/tmp/pcb_analysis.json")) print("=== PCB ANALYSIS ===") print("summary:", p["summary"]) print("stats:", {k: p["statistics"].get(k) for k in ("footprint_count", "smd_count", "tht_count", "via_count", "zone_count", "routing_complete", "unrouted_net_count")}) print("fab_notes:", p.get("fab_notes_completeness")) for f in p.get("findings", []):     if f.get("severity") != "info" or f.get("rule_id") in ("VP-001", "BV-001", "FD-001", "OR-001", "SK-001", "TE-001", "CP-001"):         print(f"  [{f.get(\"severity\")}] {f.get(\"rule_id\")}: {f.get(\"summary\")}") c = json.load(open("/tmp/cross_analysis.json")) print("=== CROSS ANALYSIS ===") print("summary:", c["summary"]) for f in c.get("findings", []):     if f.get("severity") != "info" or f.get("rule_id", "").startswith("XV-"):         print(f"  [{f.get(\"severity\")}] {f.get(\"rule_id\")}: {f.get(\"summary\")}") '` |

| 390 | List all silkscreen texts and shapes in PCB | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 layer_name = {pcbnew.F_SilkS: "F.SilkS", pcbnew.B_SilkS: "B.SilkS"}  print("=== BOARD DRAWINGS ON SILK ===") for d in b.GetDrawings():     l = d.GetLayer()     if l not in layer_name:         continue     if isinstance(d, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT):         p = d.GetPosition()         h = pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetTextHeight())         w = pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetTextThickness())         print(f"BOARD_TEXT {layer_name[l]} {d.GetText()!r:18} ({pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0:6.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0:6.2f}) deg={d.GetTextAngleDegrees():5.1f} h={h:.2f} w={w:.2f} mir={d.IsMirrored()}")     elif isinstance(d, pcbnew.PCB_SHAPE):         bb = d.GetBoundingBox()         print(f"BOARD_SHAPE {layer_name[l]} shape={d.GetShape()} ({pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetX())-X0:6.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetY())-Y0:6.2f})..({pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetRight())-X0:6.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(bb.GetBottom())-Y0:6.2f})")  print("\n=== FOOTPRINT SILK TEXTS ===") for fp in sorted(b.GetFootprints(), key=lambda f: f.GetReference()):     ref_str = fp.GetReference()     rf = fp.Reference()     if rf.IsVisible() and rf.GetLayer() in layer_name:         p = rf.GetPosition()         print(f"FP_REF  {ref_str:6} {layer_name[rf.GetLayer()]} {rf.GetText()!r:14} ({pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0:6.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0:6.2f}) deg={rf.GetTextAngleDegrees():5.1f} h={pcbnew.ToMM(rf.GetTextHeight()):.2f}")     for gi in fp.GraphicalItems():         if isinstance(gi, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT) and gi.IsVisible() and gi.GetLayer() in layer_name:             p = gi.GetPosition()             print(f"FP_TXT  {ref_str:6} {layer_name[gi.GetLayer()]} {gi.GetText()!r:14} ({pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0:6.2f}, {pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0:6.2f}) deg={gi.GetTextAngleDegrees():5.1f} h={pcbnew.ToMM(gi.GetTextHeight()):.2f}") '` |
| 392 | Inspect PCB_SHAPE geometry getters on silk layers | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for d in b.GetDrawings():     if d.GetLayer() in (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS) and isinstance(d, pcbnew.PCB_SHAPE):         st = d.GetShape()         if st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_RECT:             s, e = d.GetStart(), d.GetEnd()             print("RECT", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.y)-Y0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.y)-Y0, 3), "filled=", d.IsFilled(), "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3))         elif st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_CIRCLE:             c = d.GetCenter()             print("CIRCLE", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y)-Y0, 3), "r=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetRadius()), 3), "filled=", d.IsFilled(), "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3))         elif st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_SEGMENT:             s, e = d.GetStart(), d.GetEnd()             print("SEG", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.y)-Y0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.y)-Y0, 3), "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3)) '` |
| 394 | Verify KiCad 10 PCB_SHAPE getters | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for d in b.GetDrawings():     if d.GetLayer() in (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS) and isinstance(d, pcbnew.PCB_SHAPE):         st = d.GetShape()         filled = d.GetFillMode() != pcbnew.FILLED_SHAPE_NONE if hasattr(pcbnew, "FILLED_SHAPE_NONE") else d.IsSolidFill()         if st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_RECT:             s, e = d.GetStart(), d.GetEnd()             print("RECT", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(s.y)-Y0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(e.y)-Y0, 3), "filled=", filled, "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3))         elif st == pcbnew.SHAPE_T_CIRCLE:             c = d.GetCenter()             print("CIRCLE", d.GetLayerName(), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x)-X0, 3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y)-Y0, 3), "r=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetRadius()), 3), "filled=", filled, "w=", round(pcbnew.ToMM(d.GetWidth()), 3)) '` |
| 398 | Check KiCad 10 text angle and justify getters on rotated footprints | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for ref in ("RV1", "C2", "U1", "U2", "D2", "R1", "R2", "R14"):     fp = b.FindFootprintByReference(ref)     rf = fp.Reference()     print(f"{ref:4} fp_rot={fp.GetOrientationDegrees():6.1f} flipped={str(fp.IsFlipped()):5} \| REF vis={rf.IsVisible()} pos=({pcbnew.ToMM(rf.GetPosition().x)-X0:.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(rf.GetPosition().y)-Y0:.2f}) deg={rf.GetTextAngleDegrees():.1f} mir={rf.IsMirrored()}")     for gi in fp.GraphicalItems():         if isinstance(gi, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT) and gi.GetLayer() in (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS):             p = gi.GetPosition()             print(f"     TXT {gi.GetText()!r:10} pos=({pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0:.2f},{pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0:.2f}) deg={gi.GetTextAngleDegrees():.1f} mir={gi.IsMirrored()} hjust={gi.GetHorizJustify()} vjust={gi.GetVertJustify()}") '` |
| 400 | Compare PCB_TEXT position vs bounding box center | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for ref in ("RV1", "U1", "U2", "D2", "R1"):     fp = b.FindFootprintByReference(ref)     for gi in fp.GraphicalItems():         if isinstance(gi, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT) and gi.GetLayer() in (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS):             p = gi.GetPosition()             c = gi.GetBoundingBox().GetCenter()             print(ref, gi.GetText(), "pos=", (round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0,3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0,3)), "bb_ctr=", (round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x)-X0,3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y)-Y0,3))) '` |
| 402 | Compare Reference PCB_FIELD position vs bbox center | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") X0, Y0 = 100.0, 40.0 for ref in ("RV1", "U1", "U2", "D2", "J1", "SW2"):     rf = b.FindFootprintByReference(ref).Reference()     p = rf.GetPosition()     c = rf.GetBoundingBox().GetCenter()     print(ref, "pos=", (round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.x)-X0,3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(p.y)-Y0,3)), "bb_ctr=", (round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.x)-X0,3), round(pcbnew.ToMM(c.y)-Y0,3))) '` |
| 404 | Run KiCad DRC on current PCB before commit | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/MacOS/kicad-cli pcb drc --refill-zones --severity-all --format json --output /tmp/drc_pre_commit.json envelope_follower.kicad_pcb && python3 -c 'import json; d=json.load(open("/tmp/drc_pre_commit.json")); print("violations:", len(d["violations"]), "unconnected:", len(d["unconnected_items"]))'` |

| 416 | Verify KiCad 10 board.Remove and fp.Remove without segfault | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") silk = (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS) removed_board = 0 for d in list(b.GetDrawings()):     if d.GetLayer() in silk and isinstance(d, (pcbnew.PCB_TEXT, pcbnew.PCB_SHAPE)):         b.Remove(d)         removed_board += 1 removed_fp = 0 for fp in b.GetFootprints():     for gi in list(fp.GraphicalItems()):         if isinstance(gi, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT) and gi.GetLayer() in silk:             fp.Remove(gi)             removed_fp += 1 print("Removed board:", removed_board, "fp:", removed_fp) '` |
| 418 | Test KiCad 10 board.Delete and fp.Delete for silkscreen cleanup | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") silk = (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS) removed_board = 0 for d in list(b.GetDrawings()):     if d.GetLayer() in silk and isinstance(d, (pcbnew.PCB_TEXT, pcbnew.PCB_SHAPE)):         b.Delete(d)         removed_board += 1 removed_fp = 0 for fp in list(b.GetFootprints()):     for gi in list(fp.GraphicalItems()):         if isinstance(gi, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT) and gi.GetLayer() in silk:             fp.Delete(gi)             removed_fp += 1 print("Deleted board:", removed_board, "fp:", removed_fp) '` |
| 420 | Verify in-memory silkscreen strip and re-add in KiCad 10 | `/Applications/KiCad/KiCad.app/Contents/Frameworks/Python.framework/Versions/Current/bin/python3 -c ' import pcbnew b = pcbnew.LoadBoard("envelope_follower.kicad_pcb") silk = (pcbnew.F_SilkS, pcbnew.B_SilkS) for d in list(b.GetDrawings()):     if d.GetLayer() in silk and isinstance(d, (pcbnew.PCB_TEXT, pcbnew.PCB_SHAPE)):         b.Delete(d) for fp in list(b.GetFootprints()):     for gi in list(fp.GraphicalItems()):         if isinstance(gi, pcbnew.PCB_TEXT) and gi.GetLayer() in silk:             fp.Delete(gi)     txt = pcbnew.PCB_TEXT(fp)     txt.SetText("TEST")     txt.SetLayer(pcbnew.B_SilkS if fp.IsFlipped() else pcbnew.F_SilkS)     txt.SetPosition(fp.GetPosition())     fp.Add(txt) print("Re-added OK for", len(list(b.GetFootprints())), "footprints") '` |
